{"etymology_number":"1","lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"greatest"},{"word":"most"}],"examples":[{"text":"least common multiple","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Chiefly preceded by the: superlative form of little: most little.","Chiefly used with abstract nouns: less than all others in extent or size; littlest, smallest."],"synonyms":[{"word":"slightest"}]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"greatest"},{"word":"most"}],"examples":[{"text":"least weasel","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Chiefly preceded by the: superlative form of little: most little.","Chiefly used with abstract nouns: less than all others in extent or size; littlest, smallest.","Of two or more animals or plants with the same name: the smaller or smallest."],"synonyms":[{"word":"slightest"}]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"greatest"},{"word":"most"}],"glosses":["Chiefly preceded by the: superlative form of little: most little.","Lowest in importance or status; most insignificant or unimportant."],"tags":["archaic"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"greatest"},{"word":"most"}],"glosses":["Chiefly preceded by the: superlative form of little: most little.","Followed by an agent noun: of a person: who does some action to the smallest degree."],"tags":["obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/liːst/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/list/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":162095,"upstream_offset":752534101,"upstream_record_sha256":"e7d186981e639289e14d6a634d34a42a706204c9e76c34944253937e9ee8c911","word":"least"}
{"etymology_number":"1","lang_code":"en","pos":"det","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"greatest"},{"word":"most"}],"examples":[{"text":"He earns the least money in his family.","type":"example"},{"text":"Of all the sisters, she has the least patience.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Chiefly preceded by the: superlative form of little: most little.","Smallest in amount or extent."]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"most"}],"examples":[{"text":"Whichever queue contains the least items by the time this logic branch is reached will be executed first.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Chiefly preceded by the: superlative form of little: most little.","Followed by a plural noun: the smallest number of; the fewest."]},{"examples":[{"text":"not even the least amount","type":"example"},{"text":"It is indicative of his true character that he could not manage to show even the least bit of courtesy in such a situation.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Chiefly preceded by the: superlative form of little: most little.","Any, no matter how small in amount or extent."],"raw_tags":["in the negative"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/liːst/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/list/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":162096,"upstream_offset":752549657,"upstream_record_sha256":"71a922ee0bfc342d43c2442346a6300b8681145503eafa6c7f414b8ad2de7018","word":"least"}
{"etymology_number":"1","lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"most"}],"examples":[{"text":"My best routine was the one I had practised the least.","type":"example"},{"text":"I don’t much like housework, and doing the dishes least of all.","type":"example"},{"text":"That decision rewards those who least deserve it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a degree below all others; in the lowest or smallest degree."],"tags":["negative","superlative"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"most"}],"examples":[{"text":"It was the least surprising thing.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to form superlatives of adjectives, especially those that do not form the superlative by adding -est."],"tags":["negative","superlative"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/liːst/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/list/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":162097,"upstream_offset":752569018,"upstream_record_sha256":"7651ea9b6ab5637b3e9145389018b4994bfcf7dd6e936adbdb32135a1435f3fd","word":"least"}
